- **gitnexus-index**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/bash/gitnexus-index) · ★745)
- **gitnexus-index**: 管理 GitNexus 代码索引：创建（首次 analyze）、增量更新（status 检测过期后自动更新）、强制重建与修复（--force / --repair-fts / --embeddings）。触发："创建代码索引"、"更新代码索引"、"索引过期了"、"重新建索引"、"修复索引"、"gitnexus 索引"。 ([HACK-WU/skills](https://github.com/HACK-WU/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gitnexus-index) · ★3)
- **gitnexus-intelligence**: Code Intelligence via GitNexus Knowledge Graph. Use when analyzing dependencies, blast radius, call chains, or planning safe refactors. ([Kennyboom/BoomOpen-Workflow-Kit](https://github.com/Kennyboom/BoomOpen-Workflow-Kit/tree/HEAD/skills_library/gitnexus-intelligence))
- **gitnexus-knowledge-graph**: MCP-native knowledge graph engine for AI coding agents. Indexes entire repos into a structured graph (functions, classes, imports, call chains, execution flows) using Tree-sitter ASTs and exposes it via 7 MCP tools. Gives Claude Code, Cursor, and other agents full codebase structural awareness so they stop missing dependencies and breaking call chains. Use when vibe coding large projects, refactoring, understanding unfamiliar codebases, or before making changes that could have cascading effects. ([spoonyc/claude-science-skills](https://github.com/spoonyc/claude-science-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gitnexus-knowledge-graph))
- **gitnexus-lfg**: Use when the user wants the GitNexus engineering pipeline run end-to-end on a task: gitnexus-plan (plan depth chosen up front), a blocking gate to execute with gitnexus-work or stop, finishing with a gitnexus-review of the result. Examples: "/gitnexus-lfg Add retry support to the ingestion pipeline", "run the gitnexus pipeline on this", "plan, build and review this feature". ([RhaegarC/RgEngineDemo](https://github.com/RhaegarC/RgEngineDemo/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/gitnexus-lfg) · 41 copies)
- **gitnexus-list**: List all GitNexus-indexed repositories. Triggers: "list repos" | "gitnexus list" | "what repos are indexed" | "show indexed repos". ([Roxabi/roxabi-plugins](https://github.com/Roxabi/roxabi-plugins/tree/HEAD/archived/plugins/gitnexus/skills/list) · ★10)
- **gitnexus-no-churn**: Set up or repair GitNexus in a repository so it never causes churn — no "index is N commits behind" after every commit and no CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md rewrites from `gitnexus analyze`. Use when adding GitNexus to a repo, when the index reports itself stale/behind, when `gitnexus analyze` dirties CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md/.claude/skills, or when asked to "apply the gitnexus no-churn pattern". Works with husky, lefthook, pre-commit or plain git hooks, any language. ([nwlnexus/agent-plugins](https://github.com/nwlnexus/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/gitnexus-no-churn))
- **gitnexus-obsidian-github**: Unified workflow linking GitHub repos, GitNexus knowledge graphs, and Obsidian vault notes for seamless project intelligence ([UltronCore/claude-skill-vault](https://github.com/UltronCore/claude-skill-vault/tree/HEAD/skills/productivity/gitnexus-obsidian-github) · ★1)
- **gitnexus-pdg-query**: Use when querying or extending GitNexus's PDG control/data-dependence surface (the `pdg_query` MCP tool, CDG/REACHING_DEF edges), or reasoning about "what controls X" / "where does Y flow" / guard clauses. Examples: "what guards this statement?", "trace this variable within the function", "why is the pdg_query result empty?", "add a CDG query". ([ronsilver/agent-covenant](https://github.com/ronsilver/agent-covenant/tree/HEAD/content/skills/gitnexus-pdg-query) · ★1 · 41 copies)
- **gitnexus-plan**: Use when you need a deep, implementation-ready engineering plan for a code change — built from GitNexus graph intelligence, statement-level PDG analysis, and targeted source verification, compact enough that an implementation agent can start without re-investigating. Also strengthens existing plans via Deepen mode. Examples: "/gitnexus-plan Add retry support to the ingestion pipeline", "/gitnexus-plan deepen docs/plans/&lt;plan&gt;.md", "plan this change using the knowledge graph". ([abhigyanpatwari/GitNexus](https://github.com/abhigyanpatwari/GitNexus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gitnexus-plan) · 41 copies)
- **gitnexus-policy**: 判斷在 Buddha-skills 或任何裝有 gitnexus 的專案中，何時該用 `gitnexus analyze`（輕量增量），何時該用 `gitnexus analyze --embeddings --skills --verbose`（完整重建）。當使用者問「要不要重跑索引」「需要 embeddings 嗎」「為什麼這次要全開」「gitnexus 怎麼跑」「gitnexus 哪些 flag 該加」「indexer 跑出來怪怪的」時使用。也會在 `auto-iterate`、`maintenance-patrol`、`new-project-init`、寫 ARCHITECTURE 文件、跨 repo 語意搜尋、debug 索引異常時被引用。 ([LostSunset/Buddha-skills](https://github.com/LostSunset/Buddha-skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gitnexus-policy))

- **Git工作树**: 当用户以 Git工作树/Git worktree 名称请求在 super-agent-v3 中创建或检查隔离工作区时使用。 ([lihah111222333-cloud/Super-Dolphin-Agent-Cockpit](https://github.com/lihah111222333-cloud/Super-Dolphin-Agent-Cockpit/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/Git%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%A0%91))
- **Git工作流**: Use when cloning repositories, creating projects with framework scaffolds, or handling Git collaboration tasks like commit, push, tag, PR, release, rebase, and history search. Triggers: clone, pull, git init, npm init, cargo new, commit, push, tag, PR, release, rebase, squash, git log, git blame ([gate-guardian/skills](https://github.com/gate-guardian/skills/tree/HEAD/XingLuo_s_opencode/skills/Git%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81))
- **Git工作流与版本管理**: Git 是你的安全网。把提交当存档点，分支当沙盒，历史当文档。在 AI Agent 高速生成代码时，有纪律的版本控制是让变更可控、可审查和可回滚的机制。 ([rockyyve/skills-repo](https://github.com/rockyyve/skills-repo/tree/HEAD/%E5%BC%80%E6%BA%90%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E5%BA%93/%E5%8F%A4%E6%B3%95%E7%BC%96%E7%A8%8B/Git%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E7%89%88%E6%9C%AC%E7%AE%A1%E7%90%86))
- **Git工作流管理**: 当管理Git工作流时，分析分支策略，优化协作流程，解决合并冲突。验证工作流架构，设计CI/CD集成，和最佳实践。 ([microwind/ai-skills](https://github.com/microwind/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/tools/git-workflows) · ★97)
- **Git提交**: 为项目创建详细、结构化的git提交信息。自动分析当前分支从新建点开始的所有提交，生成变更影响汇总表格，检查问题延续性。当用户说'提交''commit''git commit''提交代码'时触发。**禁止在master/test/dev分支使用。** ([Mr-cjf/cohub](https://github.com/Mr-cjf/cohub/tree/HEAD/packages/oh-my-opencode-cohub/.opencode/skills/Git%E6%8F%90%E4%BA%A4))
- **Git提交与发版**: 为项目创建结构化git提交信息并执行发版流程（更新CHANGELOG、npm version、打tag、推送、验证）。自动分析分支变更，生成变更影响汇总表格，完成后可选进入发版。当用户说'提交''commit''发版''发布''release'时触发。**禁止在master/test/dev分支使用。** ([Mr-cjf/cohub](https://github.com/Mr-cjf/cohub/tree/HEAD/packages/oh-my-opencode-cohub/.opencode/skills/Git%E6%8F%90%E4%BA%A4%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%91%E7%89%88))

- **Git防护**: 评估并配置 Git 危险操作拦截；具体机制由 Trae 结合仓库环境选择，修改配置前需用户确认。 ([lxyhyh/operit-skills-archive](https://github.com/lxyhyh/operit-skills-archive/tree/HEAD/skills/Git%E9%98%B2%E6%8A%A4) · ★2)
- **giu-chan-khach-hang**: Xay he thong giu chan khach hang cho SME VN — phan nhanh theo mo hinh kinh doanh truoc (mua 1 lan / goi lieu trinh / the thanh vien / thue bao), bang tin hieu sap mat khach viet lai bang du lieu SME thuc su co, moc can thiep truoc khi mat, khao sat ly do roi kem bang ly do sang uu dai, chuoi keo lai chay email va Zalo OA song song, cong 3 chi so do luong voi quy tac trung thuc. Kích hoạt khi người dùng nhắc 'giu chan khach hang', 'khach khong quay lai', 'churn', 'retention', 'khach bo giua lieu trinh', 'keo lai khach cu', 'khach doi huy goi', 'ti le mua lai thap'. Không dùng cho — thiet ke go… ([huytran1120/vie-agentic-skills](https://github.com/huytran1120/vie-agentic-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/giu-chan-khach-hang) · ★85)
- **giu-chan-khach-hang**: Giữ chân khách hàng và tăng mua lại theo 3 chế độ: chẩn đoán (nhóm khách theo lần mua gần nhất, tần suất, giá trị; tín hiệu sắp rời bỏ; giả thuyết lý do); lập kế hoạch giữ chân, gia hạn, bán thêm có đồng ý của khách; đo kết quả sau khi làm. Dùng khi nghe "khách mua một lần rồi đi", "tỷ lệ mua lại", "khách sắp bỏ", "gia hạn hợp đồng", "chăm sóc sau bán". Không thuộc Skill này: tìm khách mới, chạy chiến dịch, tự nhắn khách, tự giảm giá, thu tiền. ([dangtuabm/abm-digital-workforce-sme](https://github.com/dangtuabm/abm-digital-workforce-sme/tree/HEAD/skills/giu-chan-khach-hang))
- **giuc-don**: Đại lý hối đơn đi sớm hoặc hỏi vì sao chưa đi — "đơn hoả tốc", "Grab gấp", "đi giúp chị", "khách đang đợi", "sao lâu vậy", "vì sao đơn chưa được đi". Tra trạng thái thật rồi trả lời trấn an đúng khâu đơn đang nằm; đơn "mới" bị hỏi lý do thì chẩn đoán (lệch giá / chờ bill khách CK). Load kèm skill don-hang khi tin nhắn có giọng giục, không chỉ hỏi. ([Dili-Group/open-dilim](https://github.com/Dili-Group/open-dilim/tree/HEAD/src/skills/defs/giuc-don))

- **giudice-di-pace**: Explain justice-of-peace cases with value limits. Use when asked giudice di pace, small claims Italy. ([terzastella/italy-work-skills](https://github.com/terzastella/italy-work-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/giudice-di-pace) · ★2)
- **giurisprudenza-lampo**: Ricerca giurisprudenziale rapida per ottenere un quadro di orientamento iniziale su un tema giuridico. Si attiva con "giurisprudenza-lampo [tema]", "cerca giurisprudenza su [tema]", "sentenze su [tema]", "pronunce su [tema]", "orientamento giurisprudenziale su [tema]", o qualsiasi variante che chieda di cercare sentenze, pronunce, massime, orientamenti giurisprudenziali o precedenti su un argomento giuridico. Usa questa skill anche quando l'utente dice "cosa dice la giurisprudenza su", "ci sono sentenze su", "precedenti su", "come si è espressa la Cassazione/il TAR/il Consiglio di Stato su". ([lawve-ai/awesome-legal-skills](https://github.com/lawve-ai/awesome-legal-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/giurisprudenza-lampo-giovanna-panucci) · ★963 · 2 copies)
- **giurisprudenza-lampo**: 快速判例研究，以就某一法律主题获得初步的定向框架。触发词为 "giurisprudenza-lampo [tema]"、"cerca giurisprudenza su [tema]"、 "sentenze su [tema]"、"pronunce su [tema]"、"orientamento giurisprudenziale su [tema]"， 或任何要求就某一法律议题搜索判决、裁定、判例要旨、判例导向或先例的变体表达。 当用户说 "cosa dice la giurisprudenza su"、"ci sono sentenze su"、"precedenti su"、 "come si è espressa la Cassazione/il TAR/il Consiglio di Stato su" 时，也使用本技能。 ([CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub](https://github.com/CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub/tree/HEAD/skills/giurisprudenza-lampo) · ★37)
